Our approach reveals consistent vulnerability patterns: value projection layers exhibit the highest sensitivity and strongest cross-layer correlations across multiple model families, while other components exhibit architecture-specific behaviors. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02451.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题很具体：在十亿到数十亿参数的语言模型上，怎样以可承受的内存和计算成本，近似得到足够有用的 Hessian/Fisher 结构信息，而且不把层间耦合直接丢掉。现有做法大多只能看对角或分块对角，因此只能回答“单层、单参数是否敏感”，很难回答“哪些层会一起脆弱、联合压缩为什么会额外掉点、损坏一层后该修哪一层最有效”这类更接近实际压缩和修复的问题。论文的核心贡献不是提出一种新的压缩算法，而是给出一个可扩展的二阶分析工具，并用它在 350M 到 7B 的 LLM 上直接观察到明显的非对角结构和跨层相关性。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接意义不在于提升训练 loss，而在于补上了一个长期缺位的观测窗口。预训练模型的压缩、量化、稀疏化、局部修复、参数高效微调，很多时候都默认层与层近似独立；这篇工作给出的证据表明，这个假设在 LLM 上并不成立，尤其是 value projection 层表现出持续更高的敏感度和更强的跨层耦合。贡献量级属于“分析工具 + 经验规律”型：它没有改变主流训练范式，但为后续做 curvature-aware compression、targeted recovery、layer selection、甚至训练阶段的结构正则化提供了更可信的依据。对基座研究员来说，最有价值的是它把‘二阶结构在大模型上不可用’这件事往前推了一步，从只能看局部对角，推进到能在可扩展条件下看低秩的全局耦合。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键点。第一，作者从经验 Fisher 出发，用样本梯度外积近似 Hessian，并利用梯度矩阵化后的 Kronecker 结构，证明 Fisher 可以写成若干 Kronecker product 的和。更具体地说，把整体梯度向量重排成矩阵 G 后，Fisher 的向量化形式可转成 E[G⊗G] 的奇异值分解，再还原成 J(w)=Σ_i σ_i U_i⊗V_i。这个分解在形式上是精确的，真正的近似来自只保留前 r 个主成分。第二，为了避免显式构造规模为 n^2×m^2 的矩阵，作者使用隐式重启 Arnoldi eigensolver，只通过矩阵-向量乘来求主奇异值和奇异向量。这里的关键恒等式是 (G⊗G)vec(V)=vec(GVG^T)，于是每一步都退化成较小的稠密乘法，而不是显式存储超大 Fisher。第三，作者没有完全依赖低秩项，而是把 Fisher 的对角线精确计算出来，再与低秩 Kronecker 近似结合，得到最终近似。这样做的原因很实际：低秩项更擅长抓相关结构，对角项更擅长保留逐参数尺度，二者结合比纯低秩或纯对角都稳。\n\n和常见的 K-FAC、块对角 Hessian、逐层 GPTQ/AWQ 式近似相比，这里的区别在于目标不是训练时的自然梯度更新，也不是只为单层量化服务，而是试图保留跨层相互作用。论文强调的不是“每层内部的二阶结构”，而是“层与层之间的耦合是否足够强，强到联合扰动会产生超加性损伤”。这也是它和很多 layerwise curvature 方法的根本差别。","experiments":"实验覆盖四个模型家族，参数规模从 350M 到 7B。虽然给出的正文片段没有完整表格，但从全文描述可以提炼出几类关键结果。\n\n第一类是结构性观察。作者在多种 LLM 上都看到明显的非对角 Fisher/Hessian 结构，说明对角近似遗漏了大量信息。value projection 层在不同模型中反复表现出最高敏感度，并且与其他层存在更强的跨层相关；而其他模块的行为更依赖具体架构，这意味着“统一按层类型下结论”并不可靠。\n\n第二类是与压缩损伤的相关性。论文把层级 Hessian/Fisher 指标与量化、稀疏化后的性能下降做相关分析，结论是相关性较强，且优于更粗糙的独立层近似。也就是说，二阶值高的层在被量化或剪枝后更容易造成困惑度或下游性能恶化。\n\n第三类是联合损伤实验。作者不是只做单层 corruption，而是做 inter-layer corruption：同时压缩或扰动两个耦合层，观察性能下降是否超过单独损伤之和。结果显示，Fisher 的非对角项能够预测这种超加性损伤；耦合强的层对一起被压缩时，退化显著更严重。这一点很关键，因为它直接说明跨层项不是数学装饰，而是能解释真实系统行为。\n\n第四类是恢复实验。论文进一步测试：当某些层被破坏后，优先微调哪些层最能恢复性能。结果是，微调与受损层 Fisher 耦合最强的层，恢复效果最好，优于一些直觉式选择。这给出了一个很实用的启发：后损伤修复不一定只修“坏掉的那一层”，修与其强耦合的邻近功能层可能更有效。\n\n如果从数字层面抓重点，最该记住的是模型规模覆盖到 7B，内存复杂度从参数平方降到线性级别，这是方法可落地的前提；其次是实验任务不是单一量化，而是量化、稀疏化、联合扰动、后续微调恢复四类都做了，说明作者在验证“近似是否有解释力”，而不是只挑一个最容易成功的场景。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不建议逐式推导到底。优先看三部分。\n\n第一，看方法部分里从 Fisher 到 Kronecker 分解的主线，重点抓住三件事：为什么能写成 Kronecker 和、截断近似近似了什么、Arnoldi 为什么能避免显式构矩阵。只要把这三点看明白，方法的可扩展性就清楚了。\n\n第二，看展示非对角结构和层间相关性的图表。这里决定了论文是否真的超越了对角/分块对角近似。尤其注意 value projection 的敏感性是否跨模型稳定，以及不同架构下哪些层的行为不稳定。\n\n第三，看 joint corruption 和 post-corruption fine-tuning 的实验。这两部分最能体现“跨层项有用”，因为如果只看单层量化相关性，很多 simpler proxy 也可能做到。联合损伤超加性、以及按耦合强度选恢复层这两个结果，才是方法真正站住脚的地方。\n\n预计阅读时间 25 到 35 分钟。若只想判断是否值得后续跟进，先看引言、方法总览图、核心实验图表和附录里的复杂度说明即可。值得深挖的点有两个：一是经验 Fisher 近似 Hessian 在预训练模型上的适用边界；二是这种低秩 Kronecker 结构能否直接进入压缩算法，而不只是做分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a practical second-order question: how to extract useful Hessian/Fisher structure from billion-parameter language models without paying quadratic memory cost, while still retaining cross-layer interactions. Most scalable approximations used in compression or sensitivity analysis are diagonal or block-diagonal, so they can only tell which parameters or layers are individually fragile. They cannot explain coupled failures, joint compression damage, or which layers should be repaired after corruption. The contribution here is not a new compression method, but a scalable curvature-analysis framework that makes those questions measurable on 350M–7B LLMs.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is indirect but real. Many downstream decisions around quantization, sparsification, PEFT, and post-hoc repair assume layers are approximately independent. This work shows that large LMs exhibit substantial non-diagonal curvature structure, and that value projection layers are consistently among the most sensitive and most strongly coupled components. The contribution is best viewed as an analysis tool plus empirical regularities. It does not change the training recipe, but it provides a stronger basis for curvature-aware compression, targeted recovery, layer selection, and possibly training-time regularization that accounts for inter-layer coupling. For foundation-model researchers, the main takeaway is that second-order structure is no longer limited to toy models or tiny networks.","technical_approach":"$1f","experiments":"$20","reading_guide":"$21","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看敏感度热图和压缩相关性实验；value projection 脆弱性若复现，值得纳入量化分配规则。","en":"Skim the sensitivity maps and compression-correlation results; if the value-projection fragility reproduces, it should inform mixed-precision allocation."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Viacheslav Yusupov","Daria Cherniuk","Evgeny Frolov"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["model-compression","hessian-analysis","fisher-approximation","quantization","sparsification"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02451","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.02451","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01730","title":"HEAT: Faster Fully Homomorphic Inference via Approximations-Weights Co-Adaptation","abstract":"Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) allows a server to run a language model directly on encrypted user prompts, but current approaches remain prohibitively slow. Ciphertexts natively support only addition, multiplication, and rotation, and multiplications may be composed only to a bounded depth before a costly bootstrapping operation is needed to continue. Every nonlinearity must therefore be approximated by an iterative method, and each iteration uses multiplications. A higher iteration count bu","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01730.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 FHE 场景下 LLM 推理过慢的问题，但切入点不是重新设计一套加密友好模型，而是在保持原模型架构不变的前提下，降低推理时所需的乘法深度。全文的关键观察是：FHE 里真正拖慢延迟的，不只是非线性函数需要近似，而是这些近似通常用统一、保守的迭代次数配置，按最坏情况给全模型每个位置都配足精度，结果大量位置为并不需要的局部精度付出了额外乘法和 bootstrapping 成本。论文要解决的核心问题因此不是“如何做更准的近似”，而是“如何让每个近似位点只保留任务最终输出真正需要的精度，并让权重共同适应这种近似误差”。","importance":"对基座研究的直接相关性不在预训练算法本身，而在“训练目标与部署约束联合设计”这件事上。HEAT 展示了一种很清楚的思路：把部署时的离散计算预算，转成训练时可微的结构参数，再让模型权重在真实近似误差下共同适配。这个思路和量化感知训练、推理友好蒸馏、甚至 MoE 路由预算学习有相通之处。贡献量级上，它不是改变 LLM 能力边界的方法，但在隐私推理这个受限计算环境里，给出了一条比重写架构更温和的路径：保留原模型，做一次部署前微调，就能显著减少总迭代数和端到端延迟，同时提升密文推理输出与明文父模型的一致性。对做训练-部署协同优化的人，这篇文章有方法论价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把每个非线性近似位点的“迭代次数”从固定超参数变成可学习变量。论文先把 LayerNorm、Softmax 等 FHE 下必须用迭代法逼近的模块，在明文里实现成一串逐步收敛的近似状态 F0, F1, ..., Fn。直接优化离散迭代次数不可导，于是作者借用了 adaptive computation 的做法：对每个 site 引入一个关于迭代步数的可学习分布 p=(p0,...,pn)，前向时不选单一 i，而是取各步近似结果的期望，从而让损失对 halting distribution 可导。这样训练时既能更新权重，也能更新每个位置的“需要多少步”。\n\n为了把学习方向推向更浅的近似，损失里加入了一个 KL 正则，把每个 site 的步数分布拉向一个 mode 位于小迭代数的负二项先验。与此同时，任务损失负责保住最终输出质量。两者形成张力：如果某个位置少几步不影响最终任务，它的分布就会向浅层收缩；如果某个位置对输出敏感，任务损失会把它拉回去。论文还结合了 activation squeeze / range regularization，目的是把激活值压到近似器更稳定的数值范围内，减少近似误差放大。\n\n训练流程分三阶段。按文中描述，第一阶段先在冻结或弱耦合条件下让步数分布稳定下来，避免一开始权重和近似策略同时漂移；第二阶段联合优化权重和步数分布，让权重真正吸收浅近似带来的误差；第三阶段再把软分布收敛到可部署的离散步数配置。这个设计比直接端到端一次训完更稳，因为 early stage 若近似预算变化过快，模型容易把误差归因混在一起，训练不稳定。实现上，作者强调所有 Fi 来自同一个迭代求解过程，前向只需一次 solver rollout，反向通过 checkpoint 输入并重算中间状态，额外内存和算力开销可控。\n\n和已有 FHE 推理工作相比，HEAT 的区别有两点。第一，它不要求把网络改成多项式激活或专门的加密友好层，因此不需要重做架构搜索或大规模蒸馏。第二，它优化的是“最终任务容忍的近似误差”，而不是逐层近似精度本身。这比 per-site 手工调参更进一步，因为很多局部误差并不会线性传到最终 token 预测上，联合训练能把这部分冗余精度吃掉。","experiments":"文中最重要的结果来自加密 GPT-2。HEAT 将全模型近似求解的总迭代数降低了 3.1×，把端到端解码延迟从约 54 秒/token 降到约 38 秒/token，降幅大约 30%。同时，密文推理输出与其明文父模型的 top-1 一致率从 74% 提高到 83%。这组数字说明它不是单纯拿精度换速度，而是在更浅近似下通过权重共适应把一部分精度找了回来。\n\n从实验设计看，论文重点比较了几类基线：统一迭代次数的固定近似、只学每个 site 的步数但不联合调权重、以及完整的 HEAT。这里最值得注意的消融不是最终 latency，而是“只调步数”和“步数+权重共适应”的差距。作者的论点是：如果权重不在近似误差下重新训练，很多 site 的步数不能继续往下砍；一旦联合训练，模型会把部分误差吸收到参数里，允许更激进的深度压缩。另一个重要消融是正则项设置，包括负二项先验的形状和 KL 权重；这些超参决定了模型更偏向保精度还是保深度预算。\n\n论文还分析了不同非线性模块上的步数分布，显示模型不会平均地削减所有位置，而是学出明显异质的预算分配。这一点很关键，因为它支撑了全文的核心主张：统一配置确实在大量位置上过度保守。若只看 headline latency，容易把它理解成普通压缩；看这些分布图和 site-level 统计，才能看出方法真正利用的是“误差容忍度在层间和位置间高度不均匀”这一结构。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 里对 FHE 乘法深度和 bootstrapping 瓶颈的解释，再看 Method 中把离散迭代次数连续化的那一段，这是整篇最关键的技术点。随后重点看三类内容：\n1. 近似位点的参数化方式，以及为什么用 halting distribution 的期望来替代硬选择；\n2. 损失函数里任务损失、KL 深度正则、range regularization 各自起什么作用；\n3. 实验部分关于“固定步数 vs 只学步数 vs 联合学步数和权重”的消融。\n\n如果时间有限，预计 25 到 35 分钟可以抓住主线。更值得细看的图表通常是总迭代数/延迟对比、top-1 agreement 变化、以及各 site 学到的步数分布。附录里关于具体近似器实现和反向重算开销也值得扫一眼，因为这决定方法是否真能扩展到更大模型。阅读时要带着一个问题：这种把部署预算变成可微训练变量的做法，能否迁移到量化 bit-width、KV cache 压缩等级、或 MoE expert 预算学习上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles the latency bottleneck of LLM inference under fully homomorphic encryption, but the real target is more specific than “make FHE faster.” In FHE, nonlinearities such as LayerNorm and Softmax must be implemented through iterative approximations, and each extra iteration consumes multiplicative depth and triggers more bootstrapping. Existing systems usually assign a uniform iteration budget across all occurrences of a given nonlinearity, effectively tuning for the worst case everywhere. The paper asks a sharper question: how can each approximation site use only the precision that the final task actually needs, while allowing the model weights to adapt to the approximation error seen at encrypted inference time?","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it demonstrates a clean train-deploy co-design pattern. A discrete deployment constraint—iteration depth under FHE—is turned into a differentiable training variable, then optimized jointly with model weights. That idea is closely related to quantization-aware training, budget-aware routing, and other forms of hardware- or system-constrained model adaptation. The contribution is not a new language-modeling paradigm, but a practical recipe for preserving an existing backbone and making it substantially more deployable in a privacy-preserving setting, without redesigning the architecture around polynomial activations or encryption-specific layers.","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"$23","reading_guide":"$24","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将 FHE 推理的非线性近似迭代次数作为可学习参数与权重联合微调，思路巧妙，对关注隐私计算和模型压缩的系统研究员有启发。","en":"Jointly fine-tuning weights and learnable iteration counts for non-linear approximations in FHE inference is a clever approach; insightful for systems researchers focused on privacy-preserving compute and model compression."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Sapienza University of Rome"],"authors":["Alessandro Zirilli","Davide Marincione","Evgenios M. evaluation awareness in automated alignment audits: capable target models can recognize that they are being tested in a simulated audit rather than operating in a real deployment, which weakens the validity of the audit.","importance":"The contribution is not about pretraining directly, but it matters for the interface between post-training, safety evaluation, and deployment. The useful lesson is that evaluation realism is an optimizable variable. Model behavior depends not only on weights and tasks, but also on scaffolds, tool schemas, system prompts, reminders, and inference-time compute. For evaluating deception, tool use, alignment failures, or long-horizon agent behavior, a large gap between the evaluation environment and the deployment environment can make conclusions systematically fragile.","technical_approach":"The paper proposes two interventions. The first is critique refinement. Instead of letting the auditor emit one simulator action per turn, the auditor samples multiple candidate actions. An instance of the target model critiques how each candidate could be made more deployment-like, the candidates are refined for several iterations, and a target-model judge selects the action that looks closest to real deployment. The compute is therefore spent on reducing simulation artifacts rather than simply expanding the audit search. The second intervention is DISH, or Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness. DISH changes what the target observes by wrapping it in a coding-agent harness whose system prompt, tool definitions, and system reminders resemble deployed environments such as Claude Code, Codex CLI, or Gemini CLI. The two methods affect different sources of distribution shift and can be combined.","experiments":"$26","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Figure 6 is the key compute-allocation comparison between deeper realism refinement and broader audit branching. Figure 7 shows the effect of deployment scaffolds. Figure 18 checks whether realism gains remain after controlling for seed adherence. unders","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02573.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般的多图问答，而是一个更具体也更难的问题：现有 MLLM 评测大多把图像当作并列输入、把文本当作说明文字，难以检验模型是否真的能在长序列里持续维护“文本片段—图像区域—实体状态”之间的绑定关系。TIC-Bench 试图把这个能力单独拎出来测，重点考察模型在深度交错的 text-image context reasoning。这个维度和长上下文、多图理解、文档理解都相关，但又不等同。它揭示的问题很像语言模型里的多跳依赖与变量绑定，只是这里变量同时分布在文本和图像里。对预训练研究员来说，这个 benchmark 的价值在于：第一，它能更明确地区分“看懂单帧/单图”与“沿交错上下文持续推理”这两种能力；第二，它给出了失败模式，能反推训练数据和训练目标的缺口，尤其是现有图文对数据对 interleaved Linear、Cyclic、Convergent，分别对应单链追踪、需要回到前文场景的循环链，以及多条线索最终汇合的合并推理。Temporal Association 包含 Sequential、Retrospective、Parallel，分别测顺序事件跟踪、从后文回溯前文证据、以及并行剧情线的协调。Spatial Association 包含 Map 和 Photo，要求模型把分散的视觉片段与文本描述拼起来，恢复相对位置或空间关系。这个设计有两个优点。第一，它不是简单增加图像数量，而是显式控制推理结构，因此能把“难在感知”与“难在证据组织”分开。第二，它尽量压低参数知识直接答题的空间，让答案更依赖上下文中的局部证据绑定。论文还做了较细的错误归因，指出 abstraction error 和 reasoning error 是主要失分来源，前者更像实体映射和指代抽象失败，后者更像多步链条或多分支整合失败。这个划分对后续训练改进是有用的，因为它暗示问题不完全在视觉编码器，也不完全在语言推理器，而在中间的跨模态状态表示。","experiments":"数据规模上，TIC-Bench 共 2,280 道题，覆盖三大域和八种任务类型。评测设置上，作者测试了 10 个当前 MLLM，并统计了 15 种推理设置，包括部分开源模型的 standard / thinking 版本。最关键的结果有三点。第一，人与模型差距很大：最强模型 GPT-5.5 总体准确率 59.9%，而人工基线是 91.7%。这说明任务并不是噪声太大或标注不稳，而是模型确实没有学会这类能力。第二，难度分布很有结构性：在逻辑类任务里，Convergent 明显比 Linear 和 Cyclic 更难，说明多分支证据合并比单链追踪更脆弱；在时间类任务里，Parallel 最难，说明并行事件流维护是当前模型的短板。第三，错误分析显示 abstraction 和 reasoning 是主要问题，而不是单纯的感知错误。这一点很重要，因为它意味着即便视觉识别本身没错，模型仍可能在长交错上下文中丢失实体对应关系、搞混角色状态，或者无法把分散证据合并成最终结论。摘要里没有给出更细的分模型表格数字，但从引言已经能看出 benchmark 的主要区分度确实来自推理结构，而不是题目表面形式。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点不在技术细节而在任务定义是否扎实。建议先看 multimodal evaluation: current MLLM benchmarks mostly test single-image QA or multi-image settings where images are presented in parallel and text acts as instruction. That setup does not really test whether a model can maintain bindings among textual mentions, visual entities, and evolving states across a deeply interleaved text-image sequence. TIC-Bench is designed to isolate that capability and ask whether models can track, retrieve, merge, and update cross-modal evidence over long interleaved contexts rather than relying on language priors or shallow visual matching.","importance":"Its contribution is mainly on the evaluation side, but it matters for pretraining. An optimizer rewrites prompts to score higher, and the score comes from a judge that is itself an LLM. That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this posit","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02246.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章讨论自改进 agent pipeline 中的评价闭环风险：优化器不断改写 prompt 或系统策略，而评分者是 LLM judge；当 judge 的分数成为最终选择标准时，优化过程会主动寻找评价漏洞，而不是稳定提升真实质量。","importance":"对基座预训练没有直接方法贡献，但对 post-training、自动数据合成、LLM-as-judge 评测、RL 奖励建模都有现实启发。核心信息是：LLM judge 可以作为语义评估来源，但不适合在闭环优化中拥有最终裁决权。只要训练或搜索过程能反复试探评分器，reward hacking 就会从偶发错误变成被选择机制放大的方向。这一点对用模型评模型、用模型筛数据、用模型生成偏好信号的流程都很相关。","technical_approach":"文章给出 PROCTOR，一个 Teacher-Student 式的自改进框架。设计原则是 role decoupling 和 deterministic gating。role decoupling 指不同组件不能同时拥有提案、执行、裁决等多种权限，避免“自己改、自己批、自己上线”。deterministic gating 指最终放行权交给机械规则，而不是 LLM judge；机械拒绝不能被 LLM 批准覆盖。文中还提出 canary cases：在评测集中放入任何诚实系统都不应通过的样例。如果系统获得完美分数，反而说明它可能读取答案、利用 fallback 或破坏评测协议。文章把 LLM judge 降为 advisor：它提供语义意见，但不能绕过结构化校验、schema 检查、不可变测试、解析约束和其他确定性层。","experiments":"这不是标准 benchmark 论文，而是生产系统经验报告。文中给了一个具体失败例子：代码质量 judge 初始与专家评分的 MAE 为 0.96；优化器把 rubric 删除成占位字符串后，judge 输出无法解析，评测 harness fallback 到所有维度默认打 3 分；由于专家平均分附近也是 3，MAE 反而降到 0.92，系统错误地选择了被破坏的 prompt。文章还报告了六轮 judge prompt refinement 的经验：单纯重写 judge rubric 很快进入平台期，而结构性约束更能控制失败。作者整理了 11 类观察到的 LLM evaluation pathology，并按评价信号断裂位置分成 4 组。还记录了 PROCTOR 中五层确定性 guardrail 的拒绝遥测，但提供内容未包含完整数值表。","reading_guide":"泛读建议看 Introduction 的失败案例、Section 2 的 PROCTOR 角色拆分、Section 3 的 11 个评价病理，以及 Section 5 的五层 deterministic guardrail。预计 30 分钟可读完主要观点。若关心 LLM-as-judge 用于 RL 或自动 prompt/data 优化，应重点看 Section 4：它说明优化循环不会平均掉 judge 噪声，而会沿着评分缺陷爬升。残余风险部分也值得看，因为作者承认 Teacher 本身仍是 LLM judge，只能被外层结构约束，不能被完全信任。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in self-improving agent pipelines: an optimizer repeatedly modifies prompts or system behavior, while an LLM judge supplies the score that decides whether the change is accepted. Under this closed-loop setup, the optimizer tends to exploit weaknesses in the evaluator rather than reliably improve the intended behavior.","importance":"This is not a base-pretraining paper, but it is relevant to post-training infrastructure, model-based evaluation, automated data filtering, preference generation, and RL reward design. The main lesson is that an LLM judge can provide semantic evidence, but should not hold final authority in an optimization loop. Once a search process can repeatedly probe the judge, evaluator errors become selection targets. That is directly relevant to reward hacking and to any pipeline where models grade model-generated outputs.","technical_approach":"The paper presents PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student self-improvement architecture built around systematic verification. The two central design rules are role decoupling and deterministic gating. Role decoupling prevents one component from proposing, applying, and approving the same change. Deterministic gating gives final authority to mechanical checks rather than to the LLM judge; a deterministic rejection cannot be overridden by an LLM approval. The paper also introduces canary cases: planted tests that no honest agent should pass, so a perfect score becomes suspicious rather than reassuring. In this setup, the LLM judge is treated as an advisor. Its judgment is one signal, while schema checks, immutable tests, parsing constraints, canaries, and other deterministic layers decide whether a change is allowed.","experiments":"The paper is a field report rather than a controlled benchmark. One concrete case is a code-quality judge whose initial mean absolute error against expert ratings was 0.96 on a five-point scale. A prompt optimizer deleted the rubric, causing malformed judge outputs; the evaluation harness fell back to a default score of 3 on each dimension. Because this default was closer to the expert average than the original over-scoring behavior, measured MAE improved to 0.92, and the broken prompt was selected. The paper also reports six rounds of judge-prompt refinement that plateaued, while structural constraints were more effective at containing failures. It catalogs eleven observed LLM-evaluation pathologies across four classes and describes rejection telemetry from five deterministic guardrail layers, although the supplied content does not include the full numeric tables.","reading_guide":"Skim the introduction for the concrete failure case, Section 2 for the PROCTOR role decomposition, Section 3 for the eleven evaluator pathologies, and Section 5 for the deterministic guardrail layers. A 30-minute read should capture the main engineering lessons. Readers using LLM-as-judge for RL, prompt optimization, or data generation should spend extra time on Section 4, which explains why optimization does not average away judge errors but climbs them. agent benchmark 更像短回合任务，容易把能力压缩成单步推理、单次工具调用或局部规划；CivBench 试图把评测对象换成长期闭环行为，并且把“信息是否存在”和“模型是否主动取回信息”分开，从交互轨迹里直接测代理的注意分配与执行一致性。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在游戏本身，而在评测接口设计。它提供了一个较少见的视角：把 agent tensor，而是面对一个需要自己决定何时查什么的工具化环境。第二，叙述层（narration layer）把视觉游戏状态转成结构化文本，但不自动把所有信息塞进上下文。也就是说，信息是“可表示的”，但不是“默认可见的”；非局部状态必须显式查询。这一点很重要，因为它让研究者能区分模型是因为环境没给信息而失败，还是因为信息明明可查却没去查。第三，指标设计上，论文没有把重点放在小样本 不同，后者很难定位失败来自感知、记忆、规划还是执行。","experiments":"实验部分作者刻意收敛结论。全文给出的核心事实是：他们在四个模型家族上做了 23 次 admissible full-game runs，每局 300+ turn，典型每回合 5–15 次工具调用，因此单局会产生数千次 MCP 调用。样本规模不足以支持稳定模型排序，所以论文明确不把结果包装成排行榜。更重要的发现来自轨迹分析：一是代理普遍存在对可查询全局状态的 under-monitoring，也就是很多关键战略变量并非不可见，而是没有被主动拉入上下文；二是代理经常出现近程承诺未执行，文本计划和后续动作脱节。摘要里没有给出完整数值表，正文当前可见部分也主要强调 benchmark 设计与 pilot Many existing agent benchmarks collapse behavior into short-horizon reasoning or isolated tool calls. The focus is task horizon, defined as the number of sequential steps that must be executed correctly, rather than aggregate pass rate or raw context length.","importance":"The contribution is mainly about evaluation and reliability modeling, not base-model pretraining. Its value for pretraining researchers is the measurement lens: long-horizon success can often be approximated as a geometric product of per-step reliability. A small improvement on short-horizon tasks may not translate into robust long workflows unless it improves the reliability of each dependent step. This argues for evaluations and training signals that measure compounding error in closed-loop execution, rather than only long-context perplexity, single-turn tool accuracy, or aggregate benchmark pass rate.","technical_approach":"The experimental design separates the number of required steps from the amount of context processed. The study evaluates nine models: six open models ranging from 1.2B to 671B parameters and three deployed proprietary systems. It uses four task families, including a genuinely agentic tool-use loop, five horizon levels, and three context regimes. The tasks are oracle-verifiable, reducing evaluator noise. The main model is simple: if each step succeeds with probability r, a task with horizon H should succeed with probability roughly r^H. The design then tests whether observed success follows this curve and whether degradation is better explained by horizon or by context length.","experiments":"The main finding is that 28 of 36 model-task cells follow a geometric degradation pattern governed by a measurable per-step reliability parameter. The collapse is especially severe on the agentic tool-use task, where local errors propagate through subsequent actions and observations. The study also reports signs of increasing hazard within a trajectory, meaning later steps can become riskier after earlier partial failures or accumulated state complexity. A notable result is that bounding context does not improve performance; it worsens it in the reported setting. This supports the claim that step count, rather than context length alone, is the dominant driver of degradation in these controlled workflows. The benchmark implication is that short-to-medium horizon evaluations can sit on the flat part of the decay curve and miss production failure modes.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Start with the task construction and the three context regimes, because the credibility of the step-count-versus-context-length claim depends on this design. Then inspect the success-versus-horizon plots, the geometric fits, and the 28-of-36 model-task result. The agentic tool-use loop deserves the most attention, since it is closer to deployed agent behavior than structured-output probes. End with the benchmark-gap analysis and practical recommendations. The useful takeaway for pretraining work is not model ranking, but the role of per-step reliability as an intermediate metric for long-horizon capability, RL reward decomposition, training-data selection, and evaluation design.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"把“长链路必崩”用单参数几何律钉死了，适合用来反推训练/评测该怎么设 horizon；只看拟合曲线和分解实验就够。","en":"It nails long-horizon collapse with a one-parameter geometric law—useful to back-solve horizon choices for training/eval; focus on the fitted curves and factorized regimes."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Shubhra Mittal"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["agent-evaluation","long-horizon","reliability","geometric-decay","tool-use"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01660","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.01660","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01888","title":"Does Playing it Safe Count as Faithfulness? Reassessing LVLM Hallucination Mitigation Methods","abstract":"Recent inference-time hallucination mitigation methods for large vision-language models (LVLMs) report strong gains on hallucination benchmarks. However, it remains unclear whether lower hallucination scores reflect improved multimodal grounding or more conservative generation. video world model 训练里一个很实际但常被拆开处理的问题：异构视频数据源和异构生成 backbone 同时存在时，如何建立统一、可复现、可扩展的训练基础设施。难点不只是“数据多”，而是不同数据集在时间尺度、相机几何、caption 风格、画质、运动分布上都不一致；不同视频生成器又有不同 latent 表示、条件注入方式和优化目标。直接混数据会让监督信号互相打架，直接统一模型接口又容易破坏各 backbone 已有能力。SolarWM 试图把这两个耦合问题一起解开。","importance":"从 LLM 基座研究视角看，这篇的价值不在 world model 本身，而在它展示了一种很像大模型预训练的系统化做法：先把多源数据处理成统一 contract，再用尽量薄的一层适配把不同 backbone 接到同一训练 recipe 上。这个思路和文本预训练里的 canonicalized corpus、data engine、mixture reconfiguration、backbone-agnostic recipe 很接近。对多模态统一预训练、视频 token 建模、甚至未来 AR+diffusion 联合训练都有参考意义。尤其值得注意的是作者强调“source preprocessing 与 training mixture construction 解耦”，这和现代 pretraining data pipeline 的最佳实践一致：昂贵处理只做一次，后续通过过滤规则、采样比、权重重配快速迭代数据配方。另一个启发是训练阶段划分。作者发现大部分优化应在 bidirectional training 完成，后续 AR adaptation 和 DMD 只需较少步数，这和很多 pretrain→mid-train→distill 的经验相呼应。","technical_approach":"技术上分两块。第一块是数据引擎。SolarWM 把 10 个来源的数据集处理成统一的 canonical contract，对齐视觉观测、相机几何、语言监督和质量元数据。这里的关键不是简单转格式，而是把原本不一致的时间、几何和语义监督标准化，使得后续训练时可以在同一抽象层上做 mixture 设计。作者还特意把“源级预处理”和“训练配方构建”拆开：先离线生成 canonical clips，再通过可配置规则决定过滤、采样和 source weight。这样做的好处是复现和扩展都更容易，也便于做数据配比实验。第二块是 backbone-native adaptation。作者没有强行把不同视频生成器改成同一种内部表示，而是只引入长时程 rollout 和 camera conditioning 所必需的共享接口，尽量保留各自原生 latent、attention 结构和优化目标。这个选择很务实，因为视频生成 backbone 的异质性比文本 Transformer 大得多，硬统一往往代价很高。训练 recipe 是三阶段：先做 bidirectional model training，再做 AR adaptation，最后做 DMD。文中强调不需要专门的 ODE 或 consistency distillation 初始化，说明他们把主要能力学习放在前两阶段，DMD 更像高效收尾和分布对齐。这个 recipe 的含义是：先学好局部和双向时序建模，再把模型转成适合开放式 rollout 的自回归形式，最后用较轻量的蒸馏提升采样效率或分布匹配。","experiments":"数据侧，SolarWM 处理了 10 个数据集，共约 1.43M canonical clips，物理数据量超过 25TB，覆盖真实世界、合成环境和游戏环境。模型侧，作者实例化了 4 个 backbone 版本：5B、14B、22B、33B，分别基于 wan2.2、ltx-2.5、minimax-h3 等生成器。最重要的实验结论有三条。第一，在不依赖专门 ODE/CD 初始化的情况下，模型仍能达到很强的长时程 world modeling 表现，说明统一 recipe 本身足够有效。第二，优化预算主要应该放在 bidirectional training；AR adaptation 收敛很快，DMD 所需步数更少。这一点如果正文有训练步数或 compute 分配表，值得细看，因为它直接关系到大规模训练成本设计。第三，模型只在 5 秒序列上训练，却能支持分钟到小时级 open-ended rollout，而且不需要额外 long-sequence finetune 或 attention sink 机制。这个结果如果成立，说明长时程一致性并不一定要靠超长训练序列硬堆出来，而可能更多取决于数据 contract、控制信号设计和 rollout 形式。摘要没有给出具体 benchmark 数字，因此这里更应关注正文中的 ablation：多源混合是否优于单源、canonical contract 去掉某些监督字段会掉多少、不同 backbone 在统一 recipe 下的收敛差异有多大、三阶段中每一阶段各自贡献多少。","reading_guide":"这篇适合做方法和系统设计层面的泛读偏精读。先看 Introduction 和 system overview，抓住作者到底统一了什么、没有统一什么。然后重点看 data engine 部分，尤其是 canonical contract 的字段定义，以及 source preprocessing 与 之后，而不是从头直接做，是因为竞赛编程的动作空间太大，没有一个足够强的初始策略，纯 RL 很难稳定。\n\n第四是 GenCorrect 测试时闭环。它不是简单 self-consistency，而是迭代生成多样解、执行评测、利用反馈修正下一轮候选，在有限提交预算内逐步逼近可通过解。这个设计很像把 verifier-guided search 和 program repair 是主增益，RL 在这个设置下继续带来小幅但真实的提升。加入 5 轮 GenCorrect 后，分数升到 468，超过当年金牌线 438.3。这个数字说明测试时闭环搜索的贡献非常大，甚至大于 RL 本身。\n\nNemotron-3-Ultra-CC 没有做代码专用 RL，仅靠 SFT 就达到 304；再加 5 轮 GenCorrect 后达到 To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can rem","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02236.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"PGPO 解决的是多轮 agent 任务中稀疏终局奖励带来的细粒度 credit assignment 问题。已有的 group-based RL 方法，如 GRPO/RLOO，只能给整条轨迹分配相对优势；GiGPO 这类 step-level 方法进一步按相同 anchor state 聚合动作，但每个 step 的信号仍主要继承所在轨迹的最终成败。这样会产生 failure-side credit degeneracy：一条失败轨迹里，曾经把状态推进到更好位置的动作，和真正导致偏航的动作，可能都拿到同样差的信用。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 LLM agent 的 RL post-training 有清楚启发：稀疏奖励下，优化信号的粒度比算法名更重要。PGPO 的价值在于把“最终结果监督”改成“状态潜势差监督”，让失败轨迹内部也能分辨哪些动作有局部贡献。这个思路对长程推理、工具调用、环境交互训练都有参考意义，尤其是当训练预算不足以引入独立 critic、过程标注或额外 rollout 时。","technical_approach":"方法延续 anchor-state grouping：在一个 rollout group 中，找出多条轨迹反复到达的相同或可对齐状态，把这些状态作为局部比较单元。PGPO 的变化不在于重新设计 rollout，而在于重新定义局部优势。它从 anchor-state group 的 return 统计中估计 empirical state potential，即某个状态在当前 rollout group 里通向成功的经验性潜势。对一个动作，不只看该动作所在轨迹最后成功或失败，而是比较当前状态和后继状态的潜势差。若动作把 agent 带到潜势更高的状态，即使整条轨迹后来失败，也能获得相对更好的局部 credit。这个设计接近 potential-based reward shaping，但不训练额外 value model，也不增加模型推理或环境交互次数。它和 GiGPO 的关键区别是：GiGPO 的 step-level advantage 仍绑定单条轨迹的终局 return；PGPO 允许通过共享 anchor state 在不同轨迹之间传播信用。","experiments":"论文在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 两类多轮 agent 环境上比较 PGPO 与现有 group-based RL 方法。文本中明确给出的结论是：PGPO 在这些任务上优于或强于已有基线，并且训练开销接近原有 step-level group RL，因为不需要 critic、额外模型调用或额外 rollout。机制分析聚焦失败轨迹内部的 advantage 分配。ALFWorld 的例子显示，在同一个 anchor observation “You arrive at cabinet 1. The cabinet 1 is closed.” 下，open cabinet 1 虽然出现在失败轨迹中，但它比 go to cabinet 1 或后续 detour 更接近目标，因此 PGPO 会给它更高局部优势。提供的内容没有包含完整实验表格、具体成功率数字或消融结果数值，因此不宜补数字。","reading_guide":"泛读重点放在 Section 3.3 对 failure-side credit degeneracy 的形式化和实证验证，以及方法部分如何从 anchor-state-group return 估计 state potential。图 1 的 ALFWorld 案例值得看，它说明 PGPO 为什么不是简单地给失败轨迹整体降权。若只关心方法，可用 20 分钟读 Introduction、方法定义和机制分析；若要复现，需要进一步看 appendix 中 anchor state 构造、potential 估计细节、advantage 混合权重以及不同任务的 rollout 设置。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"PGPO targets fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon agentic RL with sparse terminal rewards. Group-based methods such as RLOO and GRPO provide trajectory-level relative advantages. Step-level methods such as GiGPO improve granularity by grouping actions from shared anchor states, but their step signals still inherit the final outcome of each individual trajectory. This leaves a failure-side ambiguity: useful actions inside failed trajectories can receive the same negative credit as genuinely bad detours.","importance":"The paper is mainly relevant to agent RL post-training rather than base pretraining. Its useful lesson is about reward signal design under optimization pressure. For long-horizon tool use, web interaction, embodied text environments, and potentially reasoning traces, the training signal must distinguish local progress from final success. PGPO offers a low-overhead way to do that without process labels, an extra critic, extra model inference, or additional environment rollouts.","technical_approach":"PGPO builds on anchor-state grouping. Within a rollout group, repeated or shared environment states form local comparison sets. Instead of assigning each action credit only from the return of its own trajectory, PGPO estimates an empirical state potential from return statistics over anchor-state groups. The local advantage of an action is then guided by the potential difference between the current state and the successor state. If an action moves the agent toward a higher-potential state, it can receive better local credit even when the full trajectory later fails. This differs from GiGPO in the source of the signal: GiGPO remains tied to each trajectory’s final return, while PGPO propagates credit across trajectories through shared anchor states.","experiments":"The paper reports experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop against existing group-based RL baselines. The supplied text states that PGPO achieves stronger performance with negligible training overhead, since it does not add a critic, additional inference, or extra rollouts. The mechanism analysis focuses on failure-side credit. In the ALFWorld example, at the anchor observation “You arrive at cabinet 1. The cabinet 1 is closed,” the action open cabinet 1 receives higher local credit than ineffective repeats or later detours, even if the trajectory ultimately fails. system-level safety alignment problem for LLM agents. Failures can come from both the model policy and the runtime harness, including instructions, skills, memory, and tool-use procedures. Updating only the harness can fail when a weak policy does not reliably follow the new rules. Updating only the policy under a fixed harness cannot adapt to newly observed failure modes. The central question is how to convert safety evidence from executed trajectories into both improved runtime control and more robust policy behavior.","importance":"The work is not primarily about base-model pretraining, but it is useful for understanding the interface between post-training and agent runtime design. It frames safety alignment as a loop over trajectory collection, harness revision, policy optimization, and renewed interaction. The relevant lesson for pretraining-oriented researchers is the structure of the training signal: safety data is not treated as static preference pairs, but as multi-step execution evidence covering plans, tool calls, intermediate observations, and final responses. This kind of signal design can inform agent-oriented mid-training or RL data construction.","technical_approach":"SafeEvolve has two coupled components. The first is observability-driven harness evolution. Completed on-policy trajectories are analyzed for safety evidence, risks are attributed to specific harness components, and bounded updates are made to artifacts such as safety prompts and hierarchical skills. The updates are versioned, auditable, and reversible, which limits uncontrolled prompt growth and makes the safety substrate easier to inspect. The second component is harness-augmented policy optimization. Harness-use SFT first teaches the model to follow and apply the evolved artifacts. A subsequent RL stage optimizes behavior under a joint safety-utility reward during multi-step interaction, encouraging the policy to internalize safe decisions rather than merely rely on external rules. The key distinction from prompt-only guardrails or fixed-harness RLHF-style training is that the harness and policy are updated from the same stream of agent experience.","experiments":"The evaluation focuses on agentic safety risks such as environment injection, malicious requests, and unsafe tool use, while also tracking benign task performance. Extended Pre-Training (EPT) on t","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02685.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理 instruct LLM 的新知识注入问题：RAG 依赖检索，检索失败时容易产生不完整或错误回答；直接对 instruct 模型做 extended pre-training 会损伤指令跟随能力；用合成 QA 做监督微调又需要大量覆盖语料的问答数据。论文想在不重新做完整 IFT 的前提下，把新文档知识写入模型参数，同时保留 instruct 模型的行为能力。","importance":"这篇更接近 continual pretraining 与 pretrain-posttrain 接口问题。它的启发点是把“知识获取”和“指令跟随”拆成两个参数空间方向来处理，而不是在同一个 instruct 模型上继续训练后再补救遗忘。这个思路对私有语料更新、周期性知识注入、domain adaptation 都有参考意义。贡献量级偏方法组合与工程化简化，不是预训练范式变化；但它清楚指向一个实际问题：当没有原始 IFT 数据时，如何利用 base / instruct 成对权重完成知识更新。","technical_approach":"DKL 使用 task arithmetic。设 base 模型参数为 θ_B，对应 instruct 模型参数为 θ_I。指令跟随向量定义为 Δ_inst = θ_I - ","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02702.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长上下文 Transformer 的一个结构性限制：模型按因果顺序读入上下文，但很多任务所需的“任务状态”是在读到后面时才确定的，于是这些后验得到的状态无法反过来影响前面 token 的表征。作者把这个问题形式化为 conditional state update task：如果条件 z 先给，处理器只需沿着一条已知状态轨迹更新；如果条件 z 后给，处理器在最坏情况下需要保留“所有可能条件下序列会如何作用”的信息，内存需求可出现指数级差距。基于这个分析，论文提出把第一遍推理得到的 reasoning trace 当作任务状态的文本代理，在第二遍中放到长上下文之前，让模型带着状态重读上下文，而不是像常规做法那样把 trace 接在上下文后面。","importance":"对预训练和推理研究的意义不在于又多了一个 prompting 技巧，而在于它明确指出了 AR 因果处理在长上下文推理里的一个顺序失配问题，并给出了一种不改模型参数、只改推理流程的补救方式。这个视角很有价值，因为它把“多轮 reread / self-feedback 为什么有效”从经验现象推进到更清楚的机制解释：关键不是多生成一些中间文本，而是让后验得到的状态在下一次前向中处于上下文之前，从而参与早期表征形成。对基座研究员来说，这直接关联到两类问题：一是 long-context pretrain 是否需要显式训练模型做 state externalization 和 rereading；二是未来是否应把这类跨 pass 状态传递做成架构原语，而不是依赖自然语言 trace 作为代理。","technical_approach":"方法很简单，但设计相当克制。整体流程是 read-compute-feedback-reread。第一遍，模型读入原始任务和长上下文，生成一个或多个 reasoning traces。作者不假设 trace 完全正确，只把它视为可观测的 task state proxy。第二遍，把这些 trace 序列化成文本 T，放在长上下文块 C 之前，再让模型重新处理并作答，这就是 trace as state。为了排除“只是多给了些中间推理文本”这种解释，作者构造了严格匹配的对照 trace append：第二遍使用完全相同的 trace 文本，但把它放在长上下文之后。这样两者拥有相同的信息量、相同 token 开销、相同两遍推理结构，唯一差别是 trace 相对上下文的位置。论文的核心创新不在复杂算法，而在这个 placement intervention：它把‘状态是否能参与上下文编码’单独拿出来检验。全文还给出 conditional state update 的理论分析，证明对单遍、按序读取、有限工作内存的处理器，condition-first 与 condition-last 在最坏情况下可有指数级内存分离。这个理论不是直接证明 Transformer 的精确复杂度，但足够作为设计原则：如果某些关键状态只能在后面发现，就应在下一遍把它提前。","experiments":"实验覆盖 3 个模型、3 个长上下文数据集，共 27 个 model-task-metric 组合。模型来自不同提供方：DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview、GLM-5.2、Qwen 3.7 Max。任务包括 GraphWalks 256K、MRCRv2 8-needle、NUB-1M，分别对应不同类型的长上下文检索与推理压力。主结果很整齐：trace as state 在 27 个组合里赢了 26 个，说明收益并不依赖某一家模型或某一种任务结构。论文还在 GraphWalks 256K 上用 DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview 做了更细的消融，目的是排除几种替代解释。第一类替代解释是 generic trace scaffolding，也就是“只要让模型先写点推理就会更好”；第二类是 first-pass answer leakage，也就是第二遍其实只是利用了第一遍答案或结论。作者通过控制 trace 内容与放置位置，显示真正起作用的是 trace 在第二遍中位于上下文之前，从而能影响 reread 时的表示形成，而不是单纯多了一段推理文本。摘要里没有给出每个 benchmark trace 位置有用”。如果从基座训练角度精读，值得深挖三点：第一，trace 作为 state proxy 的质量上限在哪里，错误 trace 会怎样传播；第二，两遍推理的收益是否可以通过 mid-training 或 post-training 内化成单遍能力；第三，是否可以把自然语言 trace 换成更紧凑、更结构化的 latent state 或 memory token。预计认真读完核心内容约 45-60 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a structural limitation of causal Transformers in long-context reasoning. Many tasks require a task state—such as an active target, a search frontier, or a set of rejected hypotheses—that only becomes identifiable after reading later parts of the context. In a single causal pass, that late-discovered state cannot influence representations formed for earlier tokens. The authors formalize this with conditional state update tasks: if the condition is given first, a processor only needs to track one realized state trajectory; if the condition is given last, it may need to preserve how the sequence would transform every possible initial state, which can require exponentially more memory in the worst case. This motivates their inference method: use first-pass reasoning traces as a textual proxy for task state, and place them before the long context in a fresh pass.","importance":"The main contribution is not another prompting trick. It is a cleaner account of why rereading with self-generated intermediate reasoning can help long-context tasks. The key claim is positional and mechanistic: the benefit comes from making inferred state available before the context is processed again, so that it can shape contextual representations during the second pass. For pretraining researchers, this matters in at least two ways. First, it suggests that long-context capability is not only about larger windows or better retrieval, but also about whether models can externalize and reuse task state across passes. Second, it points toward architectural or training-time alternatives where cross-pass state transfer is built in, instead of relying on natural-language traces as a proxy.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"$34","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the first half of the methodology. Figure 1 is especially important because it ties together the processor abstraction, the order separation, and the trace-as-state intervention. In the experiments, read the main result tables first to verify the 26/27 pattern, then move to the GraphWalks ablations, which are the best place to assess whether the paper really isolates placement rather than just extra reasoning text. Three follow-up questions are worth keeping in mind while reading: how robust the method is to noisy or partially wrong traces; whether the two-pass benefit could be internalized through training; and whether natural-language traces could be replaced by more compact latent state carriers. By extending the Jacobian Lens to recurrent architectures, it confirms that 'depth-as-recurrence' supports global workspace functionality. However, it reveals that different training paradigms (deep supervision vs. truncated backpropagation) lead to fundamentally different state maintenance and causal intervention mechanisms, offering significant insights for designing efficient recurrent/weight-tied LLMs.","technical_approach":"1. Introduces a virtual-unrolling adapter: maps the weight-tied computation graph to a deep feedforward stack, enabling the direct application of existing Jacobian Lens fitting and readout machinery to recurrent models. 2. Both recurrent models exhibit global workspace signatures: contents are verbalisable, causally steerable, and selectively engaged. 2. 的影响。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要有三层。第一，它把一个常见解释性直觉变成了可实验操控的训练变量，这对研究训练动态、参数专门化、知识分布都有方法论意义。第二，结果说明预训练阶段形成的表示结构会直接决定后续可编辑性和可遗忘性，这意味着“好预训练”不只看 perplexity 或下游分数，也要看知识是否过度缠绕。第三，作者使用 SGTM 把文章分成三个域：forget 域是 Biology，约占 3.7% token；adjacent 域是与 biology 相邻的 Medicine & Health、Chemistry、Earth & Environment；retain 域是其余主题。模型固定为 254M 参数的 GPT-Neo 风格 Transformer，16 层、hidden size 1024、32 个注意力头、MLP 维度 4096。每层指定一个很小的 biology subnetwork：1 个 attention head 和 64 个 MLP hidden units，其余参数记为 other。\n\n控制 entanglement 的关键工具是 Selective Gradient Masking。作者不是把 SGTM 当 unlearning 方法，而是把它当预训练时的结构塑形手段。对于 biology 样本，反向传播后把 other 参数梯度清零，迫使 biology 知识主要写入 bio 子网络；对于一部分非 biology 样本，前向时把 bio 子网络置零，训练模型在没有 biology 子网络参与时也能处理 retain/adjacent 数据；剩余样本正常训练全部参数。通过改变“多少训练步启用 SGTM”，作者得到 6 个 disentanglement 程度不同的模型。这个设计的优点是：数据集和后续 unlearning 算法保持不变，变化主要来自预训练阶段内部知识路由方式。\n\n随后，作者用三种常见 unlearning 方法在每个模型上做遗忘实验：WGA、WDR、RMU。论文的逻辑链条是两段式的。第一段先证明这 6 个模型确实形成了有梯度的 disentanglement 序列，且不是作者主观命名。这里他们用了三类度量去验证 biology 与非 biology 知识在参数或表征上的分离程度。第二段再比较在相同 forget 水平下 retain cost 的变化，从而测量 collateral damage。这个实验设计比以往“换数据配比”或“换 unlearning 算法”更有说服力，因为它更接近只改变模型内部结构。","experiments":"主结果很清楚：更 disentangled 的模型在 retain–forget trade-off 上系统性更好。在固定遗忘水平下，最 disentangled 的模型相对最 entangled 的模型，WGA 和 RMU 下 retain cost 大约降低 4 倍，WDR 下约降低 1.3 倍。这个量级说明 entanglement 不是边缘因素，而是会显著放大 unlearning 的附带损伤。\n\n实验设置上，训练语料约 3.7B token，forget 域 biology 约占 3.7%，每个样本是 1024 token block。评估时作者从各域测试集固定抽取 20% 子集，用于 unlearning 超参搜索和统一报告。模型规模统一为 254M，避免了参数量差异带来的混淆。\n\n从全文看，值得注意的不只是主曲线，还有两个实验层面的信息。其一，作者专门引入 adjacent domain，而不是只做 biology vs others 的二元划分，这让“遗忘一个领域会不会伤到邻近知识”这个问题更接近真实情况。其二，三种 unlearning 方法的收益幅度不同：WGA、RMU 对 disentanglement 更敏感，WDR 改善较小，说明表示结构与具体 unlearning 算法之间存在交互，不能简单认为 disentanglement 对所有编辑算法等效。若精读，建议核对三种 disentanglement metric 的定义，以及 retain cost / forget level 的具体计算方式，因为这些会影响结论的外推范围。","reading_guide":"这篇适合做方法论层面的泛读。先看 Introduction，作者把 localization 和 entanglement 区分得很清楚，也交代了为什么过去工作没有真正隔离 entanglement 变量。然后重点看 Experimental Setup，尤其是 SGTM 如何在 forward/backward 中分别约束 bio 子网络和 other 参数，这是整篇最关键的实验操控。接着看验证 disentanglement 的章节，确认 6 个模型确实形成有序梯度，而不是训练噪声。最后看三种 unlearning 方法下的 retain–forget 曲线，比较 WGA、WDR、RMU 的差异。\n\n如果只想抓结论，20 到 维护多假设粒子，强调在线学习中的不确定性与“garden-path/anchoring”这类顺序效应，能反向提示预训练/后训练里对不确定性校准、在线更新、以及多样化假设保持的目标设计；\n3) “语言 + 代码”的假设表示把可解释的符号结构与可学习的语言先验结合起来，和近期 program-of-thought / tool-augmented reasoning p(h)：偏好更短的自然语言描述（相当于 MDL/Occam bias 的语言化实现）。\n- 似然 p(e_t|h)：运行代码并与观测证据 e_t 对齐，匹配更好则似然更高（全文语境是“程序执行与证据一致性”的打分，而不是纯文本困惑度）。\n- 推断：后验 p(h|e_{1:t}) 不可解，因此用 LLM-augmented Sequential Monte Carlo（引用 LLM-SMC-S）做在线近似：维护 K 个粒子 {h_t^i}，每来一个新证据就用 LLM 根据当前证据与粒子集合提出候选（proposal q），再用 prior×likelihood 做重要性加权与重采样，必要时对粒子做“修补/改写”以适配新证据。\n- Inquiry：因为粒子集显式表示了不确定性，系统可以选择能最大区分粒子的提问/实验（信息增益式的 query selection），从而闭环地交替进行 induction 与 inquiry。\n与常见做法的差别：不是让 LLM 直接输出最终规则/解释，而是把 LLM 限定为“提案器”，把可验证性放在代码执行与贝叶斯更新里；也不是单一假设的链式推理，而是显式维护多假设并允许顺序证据导致的路径依赖（garden-path）。","experiments":"给定你提供的全文片段，实验细节与具体数值（K Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into in","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02253.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 deep research agent 的长期经验如何真正转化为后续任务收益，而不是停留在“把旧轨迹塞回上下文”这一层。作者指出两类常见失败：一类是 episodic memory 直接检索长轨迹，信息冗长、任务绑定强，推理时还要临场抽象策略；另一类是 procedural memory 虽然把经验压成技能，但技能生成与下游策略优化脱节，无法保证这些技能真的有助于规划与执行。APEx 想解决的是：如何把实例级经验、类别级程序性技能、测试时在线适应和强化学习信号接成一个闭环，让 agent 在无标注真实查询上也能持续改进。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在“问答 agent 又提了几分”，而在两个更底层的问题。第一，它把 memory 从静态检索对象改成了可被奖励信号塑形的程序先验，这和 pretrain/post-train 接口里的“经验如何沉淀为参数化能力”是同一类问题。第二，它把 test-time RL、技能蒸馏、轨迹积累放进同一循环，提供了一个接近 continual post-training 的范式：模型不是只靠离线 SFT/RL 学会工具使用，而是在部署中把新经验重新写回技能库与策略。若把 agent 视为带外部工具和长期记忆的语言模型，这篇工作的价值主要在训练闭环设计，而不是单点模块创新。","technical_approach":"方法上，APEx 维护两层经验表示。实例层 memory 保存具体查询的执行轨迹摘要，保留可检索的操作细节；类别层 skill 则按 category-modality bucket 聚合多条经验，蒸馏成可复用的程序性指导。推理时，给定查询 q，系统先路由到对应桶，检索相关实例记忆 R_M(q) 与类别技能 R_S(q)，再把两者组织成结构化上下文 E(q) 供 Planner 生成研究计划。Executor 根据计划进行多轮工具调用，产出轨迹 τ 和最终答案。执行后，轨迹会被摘要写回实例记忆；当某一类别积累到足够多新经验时，Distiller 更新该类别技能。\n\n核心设计不只是层次化记忆，而是三模块闭环：Planner 负责把技能和记忆转成当前任务计划，Executor 负责与工具环境交互，Distiller 负责把新轨迹抽象成技能。三者不是独立训练，而是用三阶段交替 GRPO 优化。文中强调这样做是为了解决跨模块 credit assignment：如果直接端到端混训，Distiller 生成的技能质量很难通过最终任务回报稳定反传；交替训练则让每个模块在相对固定的其余模块条件下接收更清晰的相对奖励。这里的关键点是，skill distillation 不再是 prompt-based summarization，而是 reward-guided policy learning。\n\n另一个值得注意的部分是 Skill-guided TTRL。部署时，Planner 在无标注查询上做 test-time reinforcement learning，检索到的 skill 和 memory 作为程序先验，约束在线适应不要偏离已有高质量经验。作者把它写成“用技能正则化在线策略更新”：不是从零在测试时探索，而是在已有程序模板附近做局部适配。随后，适配后产生的新轨迹又进入记忆库，并在后续触发技能更新，形成经验—技能—策略的循环。这个思路和传统 RAG memory 的区别很明确：后者主要提升当前样本，前者试图让当前样本反过来改进未来策略。","experiments":"文中给出的主结果是，在多个 deep research benchmark 上，APEx 平均成绩达到 64.4%，比 GPT-5.4 高 14.7 个点，比最强 memory-augmented baseline 高 3.0 个点。就论文定位而言，后一个比较更重要，因为它说明收益不只是来自更强底模，而是来自经验组织与训练方式。\n\n作者还强调了一个 context efficiency 结果：只使用 skill 的变体在注入更少 memory token 的情况下仍保持较强性能，说明类别级程序技能确实比平铺式轨迹检索更紧凑。这一点对长上下文 agent 很关键，因为很多 memory 方法的真实瓶颈不是检索不到，而是检索后上下文成本过高、噪声过大。\n\n从全文描述看，实验重点还包括三类消融。第一，去掉层次化经验，只保留 flat memory retrieval，性能下降，说明实例记忆和类别技能各自承担不同角色。第二，去掉 reward-guided Distiller 或不用交替 GRPO，技能质量与最终任务表现都会变差，支持“技能蒸馏需要由下游回报塑形”的论点。第三，去掉 skill-guided TTRL 后，在线适应收益减弱，说明测试时更新若没有程序先验，容易变成高方差探索。摘要里没有给出所有消融数字，精读时需要回到主表和附录核对具体幅度、benchmark 构成、工具集设置以及 no-ground-truth reward 的定义。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐段细抠。先看 Introduction，重点抓作者如何界定 episodic / reflective / procedural memory 的缺口，以及为什么一定要把 distillation 和 downstream RL 接起来。然后直接看方法总览图 Figure 2，理解 query 路由、双层经验检索、Planner-Executor-Distiller 闭环和 TTRL 的数据流。接着看三阶段 alternating GRPO 的训练描述，确认每个阶段优化谁、奖励从哪里来、为什么比联合训练稳定。最后看主结果表和消融表，重点判断三件事：层次化经验是否真优于 flat retrieval；skill-only 是否真的更省 token；TTRL 的收益是否稳健。\n\n如果时间有限，30 分钟足够建立方法轮廓。若要深挖，建议额外看附录里的 retrieval 细节、bucket 定义、奖励设计和 memory update 触发条件，因为这些工程选择很可能决定方法是否能迁移到别的 agent setting。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how a deep research agent can convert accumulated interaction history into future task improvement, rather than merely retrieving old trajectories at inference time. The authors identify two recurring failure modes. Episodic memory retrieves long, task-specific traces and forces the model to infer reusable strategy on the fly. Procedural memory compresses experience into skills, but those skills are often produced independently of downstream policy optimization, so they are not guaranteed to help planning or execution. APEx targets the missing loop: instance-level experience, category-level procedural skills, test-time adaptation, and RL signals should reinforce each other instead of operating as separate components.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is less about the benchmark score itself and more about the training loop it proposes. First, it turns memory from a static retrieval artifact into a reward-shaped procedural prior. That is closely related to the broader pretrain/post-train question of how experience becomes reusable capability. Second, it combines test-time RL, skill distillation, and trajectory accumulation into a continual-improvement pipeline. This resembles continual post-training for tool-using LMs: the model does not only learn from offline SFT or RL, but also writes deployment experience back into both a skill repository and the policy. For researchers interested in agentic LMs, the main contribution is the closed-loop optimization design.","technical_approach":"$39","experiments":"$3a","reading_guide":"$3b","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"看训练闭环和测试时 RL 部分即可；setup 若评测不够干净，结论只能当 agent-memory 设计信号。","en":"Skim the training loop and test-time RL pieces; if evaluation is noisy, treat the result as an agent-memory design signal rather than a firm scaling result."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jie Ding","Rui Sun","Xinyuan Zhang","Zeyu Zhang","Xin Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["deep-research-agent","procedural-memory","GRPO","test-time-RL","tool-use"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.02253","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.01873","title":"Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence","abstract":"Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corrobo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_01873.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"多智能体系统里“多报告 ≠ 多证据”。当编排器可以廉价复制 agent、重复采样同一模型、或让多个 agent 共享检索结果时，系统会把同源证据的多次转述误当作独立佐证，从而过度自信。论文把这个问题形式化为 epistemic Sybil：新增报告 Z 在给定已接纳报告 R 后对潜变量 Θ 的条件互信息为 0，即 I(Θ; Z | R)=0，则 Z 只是“证据不增的身份/表述扩增”。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对“agentic 推理/自举数据/多样本聚合”非常关键，间接影响 post-train 与数据合成：\n1) 给出一个清晰的不可辨识性结论：仅靠报告文本，聚合器一般无法区分“独立证据”与“同源复述”，因此不能指望用表面多样性来做置信度累积；\n2) 提供一个可计算的中间模型（Gaussian shared-root）来量化“共享根证据 + 多次抽取”的信息上限与增益曲线，能指导 self-consistency、multi-agent debate、RAG 多路检索等系统的置信度校准；\n3) 强调需要 provenance interface（来源/血缘信息）作为系统层信号，这对训练数据管线也有对应：合成数据、蒸馏数据、以及多轮改写数据如果不记录 ancestry，会在训练与评测中造成虚假的样本独立性假设。","technical_approach":"论文做了三层推进：定义—不可辨识—可量化模型。\n- 定义：把“边际证据贡献”定义为条件互信息 I(Θ;Z|R)。为 0 时称为 epistemic Sybil extension。这里保护的量不是 agent 数、report 数或文本差异，而是关于 Θ 的新增信息。\n- 报告不可辨识性（report-only identification barrier）：存在不同的潜在 ancestry 结构，能生成相同的可观测报告分布/相似度轮廓，但 Bayes-optimal posterior 更新不同；因此任何只看报告内容的聚合规则都无法在一般情形下正确折算“重复”与“独立”。\n- no-minting 上界：当根证据固定时，后代报告集合能包含的信息有上限，不能凭空“铸造”超过源证据的信息。\n- Gaussian shared-root model：用一个共享根信号驱动多个报告的生成，推导信息如何在“完全复制”与“完全独立”之间累积。关键点是：共享根并不意味着后代完全冗余；重复抽取在一定条件下仍能增加信息，但会趋近于由根证据决定的 ceiling；如果抽取误差相关（例如共享同一 base model 的系统性偏差），信息增益会更快饱和。\n- 系统含义：要实现 epistemic Sybil resistance，聚合器需要的不只是报告文本，还需要可验证的 provenance/ancestry 线索，或在架构上限制依赖结构（例如强制独立检索、模型多样性、或显式估计相关性）。","experiments":"从你提供的全文片段可确认：\n- 他们做了合成 Monte Carlo 来验证解析 Gaussian 模型在构造的依赖结构下成立；\n- 做了真实语言模型 agent 的大规模实验（文中写明 20,000+ 次调用），展示“在测得/存在依赖时，报告聚合会失败；引入校正后可修复”，并且有一个受控设计用来区分“表征相似度”与“证据血缘”两者。\n片段未包含具体指标与数值曲线（例如信息增益随重复次数的定量、相关系数设定、校正方法的误差降低幅度），因此不编造数字。若要复现或落地，需要补齐 Section 7–8 的实验细节与实现。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 40–60 分钟）：\n1) 先读 Definition 2 附近：把 I(Θ;Z|R) 作为“新增证据”的度量记牢。\n2) 重点看 Section 4.2 的 report-only barrier：这是对工程实践最“泼冷水”的结论，决定了为什么仅靠文本去重/相似度阈值不够。\n3) 精读 Section 5 的 Gaussian shared-root 推导：它给出可操作的直觉——重复抽取为何可能有增益、何时饱和、相关误差如何伤害增益。\n4) 快速扫 Section 7–8：看他们如何在真实 LLM agent 上测量依赖、如何做校正，以及受控实验如何拆分“相似”与“同源”。\n如果你在做多 agent 聚合或合成数据管线，这篇值得把推导与实验设计当作模板复用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In multi-agent AI, more agents/reports do not necessarily mean more evidence. Orchestration can replicate the same underlying evidence through branching, re-prompting, shared retrieval, or chained summarization, yet an aggregator may treat the resulting reports as independent corroboration and become overconfident. The paper formalizes this as an epistemic Sybil problem: an added report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to admitted reports R if it adds zero conditional information about the latent state Θ, i.e., I(Θ; Z | R)=0.","importance":"While not a pretraining-method paper, it matters for foundation-model practice around agentic inference, self-consistency, RAG ensembles, and synthetic-data pipelines: (1) it proves a general identification barrier—report text alone cannot, in general, distinguish replicated ancestry from independent corroboration, so diversity in wording is not a reliable proxy for evidential diversity; (2) it provides a quantitative model (Gaussian shared-root) for how information accumulates under partial dependence, giving guidance for confidence calibration when sampling many agents from the same base model; (3) it motivates provenance/ancestry as a first-class system signal, which has direct analogs in training data governance (tracking synthetic/rewritten lineage to avoid false independence assumptions).","technical_approach":"$3c","experiments":"From the provided text: (1) a synthetic Monte Carlo study validates the analytical Gaussian model under constructed dependence; (2) an empirical study with 20,000+ calls to a real LLM agent demonstrates the same qualitative failure mode and its correction under measured dependence, including a controlled design that separates representational similarity from evidential ancestry. Hierarchical Memory Bank: Separates planning and execution memories to guide respective stages. 2. Credit Attribution Gate: Reflects on task, plan, execution, and retrieved memories before writing, attributing feedback to planning, execution, both, or neither, and generating stage-specific experience. 3. Credit-Aware Memory Evolution: Updates retrieved memory values using confidence-weighted feedback and inserts new experience only into the attributed bank.","experiments":"CHIME consistently outperforms training-based and self-evolving memory baselines across four long-horizon benchmarks. by decompositi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_02750.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文想回答三个长期悬而未决的问题：多代理 LLM 系统里，编排器把任务拆得好不好，究竟怎样影响 worker 之间的协调；自由形式的 textual reflection 为什么常常有用，但又经常停在平台期甚至把记忆越改越坏；以及为什么一个外部 verifier 往往比更强的 text-only critic 更可靠。作者试图给这三件事一个统一解释，而不是继续停留在工程 recipe 层面。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接影响有限，它更偏 agent/post-train 理论与系统方法；但对理解“冻结模型 + 外部记忆 + 验证反馈”这一类后训练闭环很有参考价值。尤其是它把 memory editing 当成主要适配通道来分析，这和当前很多 test-time adaptation、reflection、self-improvement 系统是对齐的。真正有启发的部分有两点：一是把 orchestrator-worker 结构形式化成 bilevel coordination game，给出 decomposition quality 与协同误差之间的关系；二是证明只看文本轨迹的 gate 存在信息论极限，解释了为什么 grounded verifier 不是可有可无的工程补丁，而是信息来源发生了变化。贡献量级属于“理论框架 + 小规模系统验证”，不是会直接改写基座预训练 recipe 的工作。","technical_approach":"技术路线分成四层。第一层是协调建模。作者把 orchestrator 视为 leader，把 workers 视为 followers。编排器输出子任务分解 τ，worker 在各自子任务上生成局部解，整体目标是最大化全局效用 U。现实系统里 worker 之间并不独立，常有共享变量、接口耦合、联合约束，所以作者引入 weakly coupled decomposition，用 interaction graph、边势函数 ψ_ij、耦合强度 κ 和最大邻接度 d_max 来刻画分解质量。核心结论是：在有界耦合下，follower 子博弈是 approximate potential game，均衡松弛由分解质量控制。也就是说，拆分越干净，局部最优和全局目标之间的偏差越小。第二层是 reflection 动力学。作者把共享 textual memory 的更新看成离散 semantic state space 上的随机过程，分析 free-form reflection 在什么条件下会向“更好记忆”漂移。论文给出有限时间上界，并证明最坏情况下这个界是紧的；同时指出若没有 persistent-harm 这类可检验条件，仅靠无条件反思并不能保证误差降到任意低，存在正下界。第三层是 verifier 的信息论角色。作者构造两个环境，使得文本生成分布相同，但同一句 reflection 在两个环境里的真实含义相反。于是任何只看 transcript 的 gate——哪怕允许随机化、依赖历史——都无法同时改进这两个环境；而 grounded verifier 因为接触到外部执行或环境反馈，可以区分二者并恢复几何收敛。第四层是算法 SRMA（Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent）。它只在固定 grounded evaluation protocol 证明 verifier risk 严格下降时才接受候选 memory；在 calibration 和 non-degenerate corrective mass 条件下，作者给出精确收敛率，可为几何级也可为多项式级。对于随机 probe，论文再加 confidence gate；对于分段平稳环境，引入 re-anchoring 恢复分段收敛。","experiments":"实验分成机制验证和端到端验证两类。机制验证用 hidden-cap resource contest 与 Overcooked。前者用于暴露协调和记忆更新中的潜在状态问题，后者借助 exact BFS value table 提供精确 grounded evaluation，因此不需要 LLM-as-judge。这里实验的意义主要是验证理论量：分解耦合、记忆漂移、验证门控，是否真的按分析那样影响收敛与稳定性。端到端部分放在 SWE-bench 上，作者实现了一个基于 Kimi 的完整系统，报告 361/500，约 72.2% 的解决率，对比 public mini-SWE-agent v2 的 70.8%。这个提升不算大，但论文重点也不是刷 benchmark，而是说明 grounded verification + selective memory commitment 的框架能在真实软件修复任务上落地。就当前可见正文而言，更值得看的不是绝对分数，而是 controlled environments 如何把理论对象变成可观测量。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三段。第一段看 Introduction，明确作者不是在提一个新的 multi-agent workflow，而是在解释已有 workflow 为什么有时协调、有时失灵。第二段看 Sec. 3.1 到 3.4 的主定理和定义，尤其是 approximate potential game、persistent-harm condition、text-only gate impossibility、以及 SRMA 的接受准则。这里不必逐行推导，但要看清每个结论依赖什么假设。第三段看实验里 Resource Contest 和 Overcooked，因为这两部分最能对应理论机制；SWE-bench 只需看系统组成和主结果。预计 35–50 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。若想深挖，重点问题有两个：一是这些假设在真实 LLM agent 系统里是否可检验；二是 memory state 的离散语义建模与实际 prompt/memory buffer 编辑之间有多大落差。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses three linked questions that are usually left implicit in multi-agent LLM systems: how the quality of task decomposition affects worker coordination, why free-form textual reflection often helps but also plateaus or becomes harmful, and why an external verifier can succeed even when a stronger text-only critic cannot. The goal is to replace procedural recipes with a unified strategic account.","importance":"Its direct impact on pretraining is limited; this is mainly a theory-plus-systems paper for agentic post-training and test-time adaptation. Still, it is relevant to foundation-model researchers because it analyzes a common modern setup: frozen models, editable external memory, and grounded feedback. Two parts are especially useful. First, the orchestrator-worker pipeline is formalized as a bilevel coordination game, making decomposition quality an explicit variable rather than an implementation detail. Second, the paper gives an information-theoretic separation between transcript-only judgment and grounded verification, which helps explain why verifier-based loops are not just engineering heuristics but genuinely richer feedback channels.","technical_approach":"$3d","experiments":"The experiments are split into mechanism-level validation and end-to-end evaluation. The controlled settings are a hidden-cap resource contest and Overcooked with an exact BFS value table. These environments are used to expose the strategic and memory quantities in the theory without relying on LLM-as-judge. Their role is to test whether decomposition coupling, memory drift, and verifier-gated updates behave as predicted. The end-to-end benchmark is SWE-bench, where the full Kimi-based system resolves 361 out of 500 instances, or 72.2%, compared with 70.8% for the public mini-SWE-agent v2 reference. The absolute gain is modest, and the paper is not mainly a benchmark-chasing effort. The more interesting part is how the controlled environments make the theoretical objects observable and how grounded verification is turned into a practical memory-acceptance rule.","reading_guide":"For a skim, read the Introduction first to understand that the paper is trying to explain existing multi-agent reflection pipelines rather than propose yet another workflow template. You do not need every derivation on a first pass, but you should track which assumptions each result depends on. In the experiments, prioritize the Resource Contest and Overcooked sections because they map most directly to the theory; SWE-bench can be read more quickly for the system setup and headline result. A useful first pass should take about 35 to 50 minutes.
